# Theorem alignment: HOL Light 4CT package vs Gonthier's Coq proof

Date: 2026-10-15.  Generated by round8_align/gen_doc.py from the extraction
scripts and the hand-written verdict table (see Appendix B); the
prose sections come from round8_align/notes_*.md.

## 0. Scope and method (hand-written)

Scope.  Every `Lemma`/`Theorem`/`Fact`/`Remark` and every section-local
`Let` lemma (a `Let` followed by a proof) of Gonthier's combinatorial
theories in `fourcolor/theories/proof/*.v` -- 39 files from hypermap.v to
combinatorial4ct.v, 883 lemma-level statements plus 111 Let lemmas, 994
rows -- against the theorem phrases of the HOL package `pkg/*.ml` (load
order of make.ml after the review of 2026-10-14/15; 3,069 `prove`
phrases, 62 derived/alias phrases, 27 define_type companion pairs, 19
evaluation results; see hol_thms.txt).
The proof-script files job*.v, task*.v, present5..11.v and the data file
configurations.v are skipped (they contain no lemmas of their own; HOL's
presentations.ml / configurations.ml are their counterparts), the
real-plane files only appear in Appendix A, because HOL proves the plane
theorem (FOUR_COLOR_MAP) by a different route.

Statuses.  SAME: same statement up to representation (ssreflect booleans
as propositions, seqs as lists, `=i`/`=1` as quantified iffs, section
hypotheses as explicit hypotheses or as a bundle predicate such as
`embed_context`, `sew_context`, `redpart_hyps`).  REFORMULATED: a
genuinely different formulation -- the note says how; the most frequent
case is an extra `x IN dart H` / `~(r = [])` / `2 <= LENGTH r` guard,
marked `guard:` in the note.  SPLIT: proved as several HOL theorems.
INLINED: not a top-level HOL theorem, the fact is a definition clause or
a step inside the named HOL proof.  NOT-NEEDED: no HOL counterpart and
none required -- the note gives the reason (the lemma is unused in Coq
itself, is reflection/eqType/finType/subtype plumbing, or belongs to a
proof route HOL does not follow).  MISSING: should exist and does not
(none found).  UNSURE: could not be settled by reading the statements.

Method.  (1) `extract_coq.py` / `extract_hol.py` list both sides with
their statements; (2) `align_names.py` pairs each Coq name with the HOL
theorems named by its upper-case form (522 of the 994 rows); (3) every
row -- paired or not -- was then settled by reading the Coq statement
and the HOL statement(s) found by name or by searching the HOL
statements for the constants involved (`find.py`); `uses.py` reports
where a Coq lemma is used, which decides NOT-NEEDED versus a real gap.
Wrong name pairings were corrected by hand (e.g. Coq `addcC`
(commutativity) is HOL `ADDC_COMM`, not `ADDCC`, which is `addcc`).
The verdicts live in `verdicts.tsv`, one row per Coq statement; the
document is regenerated by `gen_doc.py` (Appendix B), so renaming a HOL
theorem only requires editing the fourth column of its rows.

Revision.  The first version of this document (2026-10-11, morning)
surveyed the round-7 package.  This version was regenerated after the
round-8 code changes were adopted: walkup.ml, quiz.ml, kempetree.ml,
chromogram.ml and initctree.ml were re-proved from scratch with their
interfaces kept (new internal lemma names; round8_early/*/REPORT.md),
the deduplication pass deleted 24 alpha-equal theorem bindings and
moved ORBIT_MAP_AGREE to walkup.ml (round8_dedup/REPORT.md), the
tidy-ups dropped the redundant `x0 IN dart H` conjunct of
`valid_contract`'s triad clause and the redundant `(!u. MEM u cc ==> u
IN dart Gc)` hypothesis of EMBED_VALID_CONTRACT, EMBED_VALID_CONTRACT_
TRIAD, EMBED_CONTRACT and NOT_EMBED_REDUCIBLE (round8_tidy/REPORT.md),
and nine freed lemmas were deleted.  33 cited HOL names had disappeared;
every row citing one was re-settled against the new statements.  Six
rows changed status: gcomp_permN/gcomp_permF SAME -> REFORMULATED (the
new walkup.ml states only the glink level), fband_rcons SAME ->
NOT-NEEDED (its HOL copy FBAND_SNOC was deleted with quiz.ml),
matchg_cgram REFORMULATED -> SAME (the new kempetree.ml has Gonthier's
statement as MATCHG_CGRAM_A), ctree_leaf_table_sub SAME -> REFORMULATED
(LEAF_TABLE_SUB evaluates `count_cbit1 [ccons (ODD i) b0]`),
embedqz_arity SAME -> REFORMULATED (two dart guards added); 25 further
rows only changed the cited HOL names (verdicts_pre_round8.tsv keeps the
previous table for comparison).  Round 9 (same day, evening): CFT_REFL was
renamed CFACE_REFL and jordan.ml's alias deleted, ORBIT_MAP_AGREE_ON
was deleted, and 14 statements lost a redundant `v IN dart Gc` conjunct
(round9_polish/REPORT.md); one citation was repointed (rlinkE), the
notes of embed_functor / embed_full record that their hypotheses are
now exactly Coq's, and cface_ac_h1 moved from SAME to REFORMULATED on
re-reading (its `y` is restricted to `bc`, Coq's mono is not).

Review of 2026-10-14/15.  The whole package was reviewed file by file:
260 HOL theorems were deleted (duplicates of library or package
theorems, dead code, aliases) and 78 added (helper lemmas, renamed
internal lemmas), many proofs were rewritten, and every statement used
across files was kept.  The extraction was rerun and the 19 rows citing
a deleted or renamed HOL theorem were updated.  Statuses that changed:
cpcolor1U_correct REFORMULATED -> SAME (CPCOLOR1U_CORRECT, formerly
CPCOLOR1U_CORRECT_GEN, no longer carries the dead `cp_restrict`
hypothesis), gtree_empty_and_spec and gtr_split_some SAME -> INLINED
(their HOL copies were deleted), and ten UNSURE rows were settled by the
reviewers: gtree_partition_left -> SAME, closed_Gr / closed_Gd / clGa /
oaGr -> INLINED, closed_outerC -> NOT-NEEDED, Dr / Dr1 / Dr2 / Dr12 ->
REFORMULATED.  verdicts_pre_review.tsv keeps the previous table.

counterpart of a Coq statement, classified by roles.py (rules in that
file; roles_override.tsv for hand corrections).

| list / arithmetic / set / orbit / colour-algebra lemmas | 612 |

| definition unfolding and representation facts | 137 |

Of the 242 REFORMULATED rows, 95 differ from Coq only by a dart-membership /

that Coq itself never uses outside their own proof script.

### 1.3 Overall verdict (hand-written)

Every Coq lemma that Gonthier's proof actually uses has a HOL
counterpart: 796 of the 994 rows are SAME, REFORMULATED or SPLIT, 49 are
inlined steps, 144 are not needed, and no row is MISSING.  The 5 UNSURE
rows are all section-local `Let`s or one-line helpers (two cfmap
one-liners (ecpR1_eq, size_cpring_ecpK), the spoke ring's face cycle
`ucycFp`, `uniq_ctrenum`, `even_genus_WalkupE`) whose content is
certainly present inside a HOL proof but was not located as a
statement; none of them affects the correspondence of the theorems that
matter.

The 144 NOT-NEEDED rows split into: 41 lemmas that Coq itself never uses
outside their own proof script (connected_permN, Jordan_dual, dual_inv,
mirror_inv, cubic_orbit, fproj_cat, chordless_rot, insertE_rot, trace_rcons,
other_colors, colorable_bridgeless, sparse_rot, adj_kernel_min, cpcolor_proper,
ctree_rotlr_proper, zrange_dart, zshiftl_eq, zproper_stepLt/Rt, size_flipq,
fitp_sym, fband_rcons, ...); 18 reflection / eqType / finType / `if t is`
folding devices (clinkP, plainP, cubicP, eqcP, eqgsP, ecp_eqP, at_radius2P,
radius2P, ctree_empty_nodeP, fold_ctree_empty, ...); 24 subtype injections and
representation facts that are identities in HOL's single-dart-type setting
(z'G', WalkupI_eq, inj_snipd, codom_snipd, map_snipd_ring, icpU_inj, injcp_inj,
embd_inj, sew_tag_subproof, ...); 13 proof devices (adjunctions, `pick`,
`eCedom`); 9 lemmas of the `cpexpand` route of cfcolor.v (Coq expands the Y
and H steps into R', K, R and U steps; HOL proves the Y and H colouring steps
directly); 5 `=m` lemmas (HOL hypermaps are extensional); 2 lemmas used only by
the real-plane layer (bridgeless_dual, planar_dual); and 32
others whose HOL proof takes a different path, each explained in its row (the
`connect clink` relation, the `dual`/`mirror` genus lemmas, `plain_orbit`, the
`insertE` structural equations, the reducibility range bookkeeping,
`sort_drulesP`).

### 1.4 Discrepancies and items worth a maintainer's attention

1. Trace convention (the one real convention change, see
   DEFINITION_ALIGNMENT.md section 5.2).  HOL `ring_trace` uses
   `trace_col` = Coq `urtrace` = `rotr 1 (trace)`, so every statement
   mentioning a ring trace is rotated relative to Coq: colorable_patch
   (`REVERSE (rot1 et)` for `rot 1 (rev et)`), Kempe_treeP and
   ctree_mem_cpcolor (`rot_list 1 (cpring ..)`), contract_ctreeP,
   colorable_from_ring, embed_contract, trace_rot/trace_rev/urtrace_*.
   These rows are REFORMULATED, not discrepancies; the Kempe-tree layer
   uses `ctrace_a` (= Coq `ctrace`) so kempetree.v is SAME throughout.

2. Hypotheses stronger or weaker than Coq's (all harmless for
   FOUR_COLOR, listed so that nobody is surprised):
   - COLORING_INJ needs `h Color0 = Color0` in addition to injectivity
     (Coq coloring_inj: injectivity only; HOL's `coloring` does not use
     Color0 specially, so the extra hypothesis is redundant);
   - CPADJ_PROPER needs `config_prog cp` (Coq cpadj_proper does not);
   - PROPER_CHORD_RING assumes `~MEM (edge_map H x) r` where Coq's
     section assumes `x \in diskE r`;
   - KEMPE_TREEP and CTREE_MEM_CPCOLOR assume `cubic_prog cp` (Coq's
     Kempe_treeP / ctree_mem_cpcolor do not; CTREE_MEM_CPCOLOR_ALL is
     the unconditional form);
   - FCARD0P / FCARD1P use `FINITE s` (/ `s SUBSET dart H`) instead of
     `fclosed face A`;
   - `connected_hypermap` holds for the empty map, so CUBIC_EULER,
     CONNECTED_NCOMP, DSCORE_ROOTS, POSZ_DSCORE carry `~(dart H = {})`;
   - the `plain`/`cubic` patch lemmas: Coq's equations `plain G =
     plainb [predC bGd] && plainb Gr` are three one-directional
     theorems in HOL (PLAIN_PATCH, PLAIN_PATCH_D, PLAIN_PATCH_COMPOSE),
     likewise cubic_patch.
   Resolved in round 8: EMBED_VALID_CONTRACT, EMBED_VALID_CONTRACT_TRIAD,
   EMBED_CONTRACT and NOT_EMBED_REDUCIBLE no longer carry the redundant
   `(!u. MEM u cc ==> u IN dart Gc)` (VALID_CONTRACT_IN_DART derives it),
   and `valid_contract`'s triad clause is exactly Coq's conjuncts.
   Resolved in round 9: 14 statements of embed_prelim.ml / embed.ml /
   embed_contract.ml lost a redundant `v IN dart Gc` conjunct beside
   `v IN kernel Gc rc` (EMBED_FUNCTOR, EMBED_FULL, CFACE_AC_H1_BC,
   ADJ_PULLBACK_DISJ, EMBED_TRIAD_TRANSFER, TRIAD_NONADJ_H1, TRIAD_COUNT_H1,
   KERNEL_DISK_FACE_AVOID, FACE_KERNEL_DART, EMBED_FUNCTOR_FROM_NO_CYCLE,
   EMBED_FUNCTOR_FROM_CORE, STRONG_CYCLE_FROM_ACHC_CYCLE, RLINK_H,
   LIST_PATH_MAP_H); embed_functor and embed_full now have exactly Coq's
   hypotheses.
   Resolved in the review of 2026-10-14/15: CPCOLOR1U_CORRECT (formerly
   CPCOLOR1U_CORRECT_GEN) no longer carries a `cp_restrict` hypothesis
   (CP_RESTRICT_ANY proved it trivially true), and the step lemmas of
   cpcolor_correct.ml now have Coq's form.

3. One-directional versions of Coq `mono` lemmas: adj_icpU, adj_icpK,
   cface_icpK, cface_snipd, cface_icpA (HOL states the direction it
   needs; the converse is either a separate lemma or absent).

4. Lemmas stated only for `(num)hypermap` (the configuration maps):
   the whole ecp*/icp* layer and the cfcontract lemmas; Coq's are
   generic.  Harmless (they are only used on cpmap).  ADJP (geometry.v's
   adjP, `adj x y <=> ?z. cface x z /\ cface (edge z) y`) is now stated
   for any hypermap, but still lives in cfquiz.ml rather than
   geometry.ml.

5. Duplicated HOL theorems.  The round-8 deduplication pass
   (round8_dedup/REPORT.md) deleted the alpha-equal copies found by the
   first version of this table (SIMPLE_DISTINCT, SCYCLE_LIST_CYCLE,
   SCYCLE_FACE_SIMPLE, FACE_SIMPLE_APPEND_EQ, PERMT_ID_123, PERMT_123,
   GSUB_EMPTY, GLINK_SELF, ORBIT_MAP_AGREE_PERM, EDGE_ECPN_OLD, ten bare
   aliases, and the double bindings of birkhoff.ml) and turned eight
   corollaries into one-line derivations; the review of 2026-10-14/15
   deleted 260 more (library duplicates, aliases, dead code).  32 SAME
   rows still cite two HOL theorems; all of them are genuinely different
   forms kept on purpose (hypermap_pred bundle vs composition, iff vs
   one direction, `ctrace` vs `ctrace_a`, generic vs instance), see the
   dedup report's group table.  Round 9 renamed geometry.ml's CFT_REFL
   to CFACE_REFL and deleted jordan.ml's alias, and deleted
   ORBIT_MAP_AGREE_ON (cpcolor_correct.ml, a corollary of walkup.ml's
   ORBIT_MAP_EQ_AGREE).

6. Counterparts in unexpected files (section 1.2): KEMPE_VALID_RESTRICT
   and KEMPE_VALID_INIT (kempetree.v) are in birkhoff.ml;
   SIMPLE_FCARD_FBAND / SCYCLE_FCARD_FBAND (geometry.v) in birkhoff.ml;
   the `cface_icpY/H`, `adj_ecpY/H`, `adj_icp*` lemmas of cfmap.v in
   cfcontract.ml / cpcolor_correct.ml; genus_mirror / planar_mirror /
   gcomp_mirror in present.ml; connected_clink / clink_glink in
   revsnip.ml; the Birkhoff spoke-ring lemmas fcard_adj_adj,
   adj12_edge, fcard_adj_max in contract.ml; cpmask lemmas of cfmap.v in
   cfcontract.ml; cycle_rev_cpring in cfquiz.ml.

7. Naming hazards for readers of both developments: HOL ADDCC is Coq
   addcc (self-inverse), Coq addcC (commutativity) is ADDC_COMM; HOL
   `ctrace` prepends the checksum, `ctrace_a` is Coq's ctrace; HOL
   `cprsize` is the ring length (Coq size_ring_cpmap is a definition
   in HOL) and `cpringsize` is Coq's `cprsize`; HOL `edge H x` is the
   orbit; `trace_col` is Coq `urtrace`; `face_simple` is `simple_rec`.

### 1.5 Where the HOL-only theorems come from

The HOL files of the common theories contain 2,935 `prove` phrases
(including the 110 of general.ml) against 883 Coq lemmas + 111 Lets.
871 HOL theorems are cited as counterparts; the remaining 2,064 are
HOL-only, and section 1.1 classifies them (rules in roles.py; the
classification is heuristic, by name and file, and is meant to give
orders of magnitude):

- intermediate steps of one Coq proof (~720): Gonthier proves a lemma
  with `have`/`suff` steps inside one script; the HOL style (small
  top-level lemmas, SUBGOAL_THEN) states those steps as theorems:
  JORDAN_STEP_E1..F7 and EULER_TREE_* (jordan.ml, 105 theorems for 6
  Coq lemmas), the walkup component-count lemmas (walkup.ml, 77 for
  40 after the round-8 re-proof, 112 before), KEMPE_CASE_A/B,
  RING_CASE_A/B (kempe.ml, 90 for 4), the NT_DF_*,
  EF_*, THR_*, CHORD_* lemmas of embed_prelim.ml, the KTC_* lemmas of
  kempetree.ml, the DK_PRIME_*/K0G_* lemmas of cpcolor_correct.ml.
- library lemmas (~610, including the 115 HOL-only theorems of
  general.ml): ssreflect's seq/path/fintype/bigop and finfun lemmas
  (rot, rotr, arc, index, take/drop, next/prev, uniq, mask, iter, orbits
  of permutations, card of orbit partitions, isum) and the colour
  algebra (addc/permc/permt/trace) that HOL has to prove itself.
- per-construction-step copies (~320): one lemma per construction step
  (ecpU/ecpN/ecpY/ecpH/ecpK/ecpA/ecpR, CpR/CpY/CpH), per colouring
  extension (kuU/kuY/kuH/kcfK), per quiz step (walk_Y/walk_H), per
  contract step (CTR_CORRECT_R/Y/H, CTRBAND_*, CPMASK_ADJ_*,
  SPARSE_INV_*), per zpart location (redpart) and per discharge rule
  (SYM_FIT_*, DBOUND*): where Coq proves one generic statement with a
  `case` or a `foldr`, HOL has the instances; cfmap.ml, cfcontract.ml
  and cpcolor_correct.ml hold most of them.
- dart-membership and finiteness side conditions (~175): `x IN dart H`,
  `IN embd_D`, `FINITE (diskN ..)`, `.. SUBSET dart ..`: the price of a
  dart predicate instead of a finite dart type.
- definition unfolding and representation facts (~140): clausal
  equations for `define`d functions, `*_COMPONENTS` / `*_PRED` /
  `*_PERMUTES` / `*_ENF` for every constructed hypermap (ecpU, ecpN,
  ecpA, snip_disk, snip_rem, sew_map, embed_disk, embed_rem, cube,
  edge_walkup, sub_hypermap): Coq gets these from the record
  constructor and `cancel3` proofs.
- hypothesis-bundle projections (~50): MCE_*, CTX_*, REM_*, *_FACTS:
  Coq's section variables become bundle predicates whose conjuncts must
  be projected out.
- second forms of a Coq-matched theorem (24), datatype companions (9)
  and evaluation glue (16).

In short: the 3.3x theorem count is granularity, not extra mathematics.
Nothing in the HOL-only mass is a lemma of Gonthier's proof that is
absent on the Coq side; conversely the only Coq lemmas without any HOL
trace are the 144 NOT-NEEDED ones above.

## 2. Per-theory alignment (generated)

Row format: `- coq_name [STATUS] (Let)` for section-local Let lemmas,
then `HOL:` the counterpart theorem(s) with their file, `Coq:` the
statement (binders included, cut to one line), `note:` the verdict
note (hypothesis differences, representation changes, where a step was
inlined, why a lemma is not needed).  A `guard:` note means the only
difference is a dart-membership / nonemptiness side condition.

### 2.1 hypermap.v -> hypermap.ml jordan.ml geometry.ml general.ml

61 Coq statements: 20 SAME, 16 REFORMULATED, 3 INLINED, 22 NOT-NEEDED.

  - edgeK [SAME]
    HOL: edgeK (hypermap.ml)

          FINITE s /\ f permutes s /\ x IN s (HOL `edge H x` is the orbit)
  - cnodeC [REFORMULATED]

## 3. Conventions used in the verdicts (hand-written)

- Section hypotheses.  Coq's `Variable`/`Hypothesis` of a section become
  explicit hypotheses of the HOL theorem (e.g. `minimal_counter_example
  H`, `planar_hypermap H /\ scycle H (rlink H) r /\ ALL (\y. y IN dart
  H) r`) or a bundle predicate (`embed_context Gm Gc rc h`, `sew_context
  Gd Gr bGd bGr`, `redpart_hyps G nhub red_rec p0r x0`, `injcp_ctx`,
  the `redp`/`rf` arguments of hubcap.ml).  This alone is counted as
  SAME.
- Guards.  An extra `x IN dart H`, `~(r = [])`, `2 <= LENGTH r` or
  `FINITE s` hypothesis, needed because HOL darts form a predicate on
  a type (maps are the identity off the darts) and `list_cycle` of the
  empty list is false, is counted as REFORMULATED with the note
  `guard:`; 1.1 gives the number of such rows.
- Reflection.  `reflect P b` lemmas become `b <=> P` theorems (SAME) or
  are NOT-NEEDED when HOL's definition is the proposition itself
  (clinkP, plainP, cubicP, radius2P).
- `=i`, `=1`, `=2`, `{mono f : x y / r x y}`, `{morph f : x / g x}`,
  `{in A, ...}` are read as the corresponding quantified statements.
- Single dart type.  Coq's injections snipd/snipr/embd/embr/icp*/sewd
  are identities in HOL (except sewd/sewr into `A+B`), so their
  injectivity and codomain lemmas are NOT-NEEDED or become `IN_*_D`
  statements; `preim_seq` rings are the rings themselves.
- Trace convention.  See 1.4 item 1 and DEFINITION_ALIGNMENT.md 5.2.
- INLINED is used in two situations: the Coq statement is a definition
  clause in HOL (simple_cons, fitqa_topqa, rqs_fit_cons, precubic_leq,
  size_ring_cpmap), or the fact is proved inside the cited HOL theorem
  (even then the cited theorem is given).  Where the inlining was only
  inferred, the row is UNSURE instead.
- The HOL theorem counts use `prove` phrases only (`let NAME = prove`),
  which is how PROOF_LENGTH_COMPARISON.md counts; derived phrases such
  as `let edgeK = REWRITE_RULE ...` are listed in hol_thms.txt with kind
  `derived` and appear as counterparts where relevant (edgeK, nodeK,
  faceK, birkhoff2..5).

## Appendix A. The real-plane layer and the HOL-specific final forms

Coq real-plane files (statements, not aligned; HOL proves the plane theorem by
another route): dedekind.v 65, grid.v 91, gridmap.v 24, matte.v 34, approx.v
32, discretize.v 8, finitize.v 30, fourcolor.v 2 (total 286).

HOL final-form files (`prove` phrases): planar_graph_link.ml 27,
four_color_planar.ml 3, map_defs.ml 4, map_grid.ml 50, map_main.ml 4,
four_color_map.ml 2 (total 90).

The real-plane files are not aligned: HOL's map_defs.ml, map_grid.ml,
map_main.ml and four_color_map.ml prove `FOUR_COLOR_MAP : |- !M.
plane_map M ==> map_colorable 4 M` over `real^2` through the planar-graph
form (planar_graph_link.ml, four_color_planar.ml: hypermap of a graph,
dual colouring) and a grid-minor / compactness argument, where Gonthier
discretises the plane (grid.v, matte.v, approx.v, discretize.v) and uses
Koenig's lemma (finitize.v).  None of the Coq lemmas of that layer has,
or needs, a HOL counterpart, but the end results correspond:
FOUR_COLOR_MAP is fourcolor.v's four_color (over HOL's real^2 rather than
any model of the reals, and with a map given as a set of regions rather
than a relation on points), FOUR_COLOR_FINITE_MAP is four_color_finite,
MAP_COLORABLE_COMPACTNESS plays the role of finitize.v's
compactness_extension and ADJACENCY_GRAPH_PLANAR that of discretize.v's
discretize_to_hypermap.  The planar-graph forms (FOUR_COLOR_PLANAR_GRAPH,
FOUR_COLOR_KURATOWSKI, FOUR_COLOR_WAGNER) and PLANAR_K4_NOT_3_COLORABLE
have no Coq counterpart.
realplane.ml in the development tree defines Gonthier's real-plane
notions but is not part of the shipped chain.

## Appendix B. Scripts (round8_align/) and how to regenerate

The scripts and their data are kept with the development; they are not
part of the HOL Light package, which needs only this document.

- extract_coq.py  -> coq_thms.txt: every Lemma/Theorem/Fact/Remark and
  every proved Let of fourcolor/theories/proof/*.v (job*/task*/present*/
  configurations skipped) with file, line, kind, enclosing sections and
  statement.
- extract_hol.py  -> hol_thms.txt: every top-level theorem phrase of
  pkg/*.ml (make.ml order, then the planar-graph / plane-map files) with
  kind prove / derived / multi / compute and the quoted statement.
- align_names.py  -> thms_byname.txt: the name-based first pass (upper-
  case name, primes as _P/_ALT, case/underscore-insensitive fallback),
  both statements side by side, and the unmatched HOL theorems of each
  theory's files.
- find.py REGEX [FILE_REGEX] (-s: search statements) and uses.py NAME..
  (where a Coq lemma is used): the lookup tools used to settle rows.
- verdicts.tsv: the hand-written verdicts, one row per Coq statement
  (coq_file, coq_name, STATUS, HOL counterparts NAME@file, note).
  check_verdicts.py verifies that every Coq statement has exactly one
  row, every cited HOL name exists and every status is legal.
- roles.py (+ roles_override.tsv): role classification of the HOL-only
  theorems; stats.py: auxiliary numbers for the prose.
- gen_doc.py: assembles this document from the files above and the
  prose in notes_intro.md, notes_summary.md, notes_conventions.md,
  notes_appendix.md; it checks the 79-column and 7-bit ASCII limits.

Regeneration after a rename or a re-proof: run extract_coq.py,
extract_hol.py, align_names.py, edit the affected rows of verdicts.tsv
(column 4 for renamed HOL theorems; add rows for new Coq lemmas),
python3 check_verdicts.py, python3 gen_doc.py.
